PS C:\Users\Laco> foreach ($w in $windows) {
    Write-Output "`n--- WINDOW $($w.Start) TO $($w.End) ---"

    $found = Get-WinEvent -FilterHashtable @{
        LogName = 'Security'
        Id = 4688
        StartTime = $w.Start
        EndTime = $w.End
    } -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue

    if ($found) {
        foreach ($ev in $found) {
            $xml = $ev.ToXml()
            $match = $false

            foreach ($pid in $pids) {
                $hex = '0x{0:x}' -f $pid
                if ($xml -match [regex]::Escape($hex) -or
                    $xml -match "(?<!\d)$pid(?!\d)") {
                    $match = $true
                    break
                }
            }

            if ($match) {
                Write-Output "`nMATCHED EVENT 4688"
                Write-Output "TIME: $($ev.TimeCreated.ToString('o'))"
                Write-Output "RECORD: $($ev.RecordId)"
                Write-Output $xml
            }
        }
    } else {
        Write-Output "No 4688 events returned for this window."
    }
}

PS C:\Users\Laco> Write-Output "`n===== SYSMON CHANNEL AVAILABILITY ====="

===== SYSMON CHANNEL AVAILABILITY =====
PS C:\Users\Laco> $sysmon = Get-WinEvent -ListLog 'Microsoft-Windows-Sysmon/Operational' `
    -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
PS C:\Users\Laco> if ($sysmon) {
    $sysmon | Select-Object LogName, IsEnabled, RecordCount |
        Format-List

    Write-Output "`n===== SYSMON EVENT 1 NEAR ACTIVATION FAILURES ====="

    foreach ($w in $windows) {
        Write-Output "`n--- WINDOW $($w.Start) TO $($w.End) ---"

        $found = Get-WinEvent -FilterHashtable @{
            LogName = 'Microsoft-Windows-Sysmon/Operational'
            Id = 1
            StartTime = $w.Start
            EndTime = $w.End
        } -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue

        if ($found) {
            foreach ($ev in $found) {
                $xml = $ev.ToXml()
                $match = $false

                foreach ($pid in $pids) {
                    $hex = '0x{0:x}' -f $pid
                    if ($xml -match [regex]::Escape($hex) -or
                        $xml -match "(?<!\d)$pid(?!\d)") {
                        $match = $true
                        break
                    }
                }

                if ($match) {
                    Write-Output "`nMATCHED SYSMON EVENT 1"
                    Write-Output "TIME: $($ev.TimeCreated.ToString('o'))"
                    Write-Output "RECORD: $($ev.RecordId)"
                    Write-Output $xml
                }
            }
        } else {
            Write-Output "No Sysmon Event 1 records returned."
        }
    }
} else {
    Write-Output "Sysmon Operational channel is unavailable."
}

IsEnabled   : True
RecordCount : 38916
